LISTING 4: A NELLYSFORD NEST
Type: Cabins & Cottages
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
One of Airbnb's favorites!! 4BR/2.5BA on 5 acres in the forest adjacent to 40 acres of private eco-village trails. Centrally located near Nelson County 151 attractions, with Bold Rock right at the top of our street and Devils Backbone 1.5mi away. The PERFECT place for nature lovers, families, and friends to enjoy the best of the Blue Ridge Mountains along the Blue Ridge Parkway. All brand new furnishings. Enjoy our front porch, screened-in patio, sunroom, 'playroom' garage, and fire pit!! This is our third Airbnb -- 'three's a charm!!' We met at UVA so this spacious home is very special to us. We've poured our heart &amp; soul into designing this as an ideal spot for friends and family to getaway and and enjoy the mountain views. You're surrounded by nature at our unique eco-village cabin but also super close to the several breweries &amp; cideries &amp; wineries &amp; distilleries &amp; restaurants that Nelson County Route 151 offers. Furnishings are brand new and we've thoughtfully curated every inch of the property!! Downstairs, our chestnut leather sectional faces our large TV (65') with YouTube TV &amp; other streaming services. The fully-equipped kitchen features an induction stove and plenty of amenities to cook meals for your party. The 8' farm table comfortably seats 8 people. The sunroom windows feature pull-down screens to enjoy the breeze and plenty of natural light. The downstairs master features a king bed, walk-in closet, and ensuite full bathroom. The front hallway has a half bath. Upstairs, enjoy another sitting area with another large TV (55'), bookshelf, playing cards, poker chips, and plenty of board games. If the couch doesn't provide enough space, feel free to grab our 4 colorful bean bags off the landing. Kids love these!! We provide an acoustic guitar, so don't worry about packing your own. For the 3 upstairs bedrooms: the first features a king bed, the second features 2 queen beds, and the third features a twin-over-full bunk bed. There's also a full bathroom. Outside, we have a Weber grill on the back patio and, again, seating for 8 on our patio set with the red umbrella. We also offer a 'playroom' garage equipped with our third flat-screen TV, soundbar, ping pong table, darts board, cooler, and cornhole . In the front yard, enjoy our custom built fire pit with bench seating around our Yukon 27' Solo Stove. In the 'attached' garage, we provide a folding table, folding chairs, camp chairs, and a portable cooler. And a short walk (6min) from our property, you'll access 40 acres of private eco-village trails that are PERFECT for a morning stroll or evening saunter!! We'd love to be your next adventure!! <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Jeff Smidt, jeff@stargazingllc.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>No <strong>Located in </strong>Nellysford

LISTING 5: ACORN INN, INC (COTTAGE, B&B AND GROUP RETREAT)
Type: Bed and Breakfast
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Acorn Inn, has three different structures on the property geared to hosting guests. What we call 'The Inn,' was once a horse barn. There are 10 bedrooms (once stalls!), men's &amp; women's bathrooms &amp; showers, several lovely gathering spaces, two kitchenettes, a wraparound deck, covered patio, an outdoor grill &amp; more. (NO PETS at the Inn because it is a shared space.) Our Cottage is a cozy little house with a full kitchen, private bath bedroom with queen &amp; twin, and living room with a queen sofa bed. (We accept well-behaved pets in the Cottage for an additional fee.) Finally our Farmhouse, which is also our office, has three bedrooms, each with a queen bed. (These are only rented when the Inn and Cottage are fully booked, at the innkeepers' discretion.) We've been running ACORN INN since 1987! Martin is a well-travelled cyclist from the Netherlands &amp; Kathy is a photographer &amp; printmaker. The Inn is full of art made by Kathy and from artisans around the world. Affordable, cozy &amp; friendly. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Kathy &amp; Martin Versluys, contact@acorninn.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>Yes <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Faber

LISTING 9: AFTON MOUNTAIN INN
Type: Bed and Breakfast
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Built in 1848, more than a decade before the start of the American Civil War, our historic farmhouse features original heart pine flooring, ornate woodwork, and a stained glass entryway. Nearly two centuries later, the property has been converted into the comfortable self-check-in boutique hotel you see today. The inn features six one-bedroom guest suites, as well as a two-bedroom Premium Villa. All guests enjoy premium bedding and linens, free WiFi Internet, complimentary robes and pool towels, wine glasses, free coffee, free snack bar with ice cream, high-definition flat panel TVs, blackout curtains, sound machines, and mini-fridges. The 10-acre property features a series of outdoor amenities, include a pool (open May-September), two hot tubs, a series of outdoor decks and lounge areas, three fire pits, a badminton court, and a private nature trail. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Be Still Getaways <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>No <strong>Located in </strong>Afton

====================================================================================================
LISTING 14: APPALACHIAN TRAIL FROM RT 56 TO HARPERS CREEK
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<div class='fusion-text fusion-text-1 fusion-text-no-margin'> <strong>Miles:</strong> 2.6 <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Moderate </div> A 2.6-mile moderate hike along the Appalachian Trail traverses a wooden Swinging Bridge over the Tye River, and travels through stands of mountain laurel and rhododendron to the Harpers Creek Shelter (AT) and Harper’s Creek, where the water gurgles through the large cascading rocks. Multiple campsites are on each side of the creek, and there’s a vault toilet up the hill from the shelter. <strong>Directions </strong>From State Route 56 at Crabtree Falls: Take SR 56 east for approximately 3-4 miles. The Appalachian Trail crosses SR 56 and a AT parking lot is available to the right (South side) of SR 56. Head across the street from the parking lot (North) on the AT across the Swinging Bridge, and continue up the trail towards Harper’s Creek Shelter.<strong> </strong>

AFTER (Cleaned & Formatted):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

BEFORE (Original Content):

For almost 10 years Avon Hill Lodge has offered a unique getaway, close to the heart of Virginia wine country. Tucked away in the rolling hills of Nelson County, the property has served as an exclusive vacation destination and retreat for families, groups, and businesses. Located 28 miles south of Charlottesville, Avon Hill provides a serene escape with views of the Blue Ridge Mountains. The Avon Hill estate is made up of 100 tree lined acres along the Rockfish River, over five acres of English and American Boxwoods, four overnight accommodations buildings, a pool, and as of 2019, Bruce’s Barn. With the addition of Bruce’s Barn, Avon Hill Lodge can host private events for 150 and makes the perfect setting for your destination wedding. Avon Hill offers unprecedented access to local Charlottesville attractions, while maintaining complete privacy in its serene and peaceful countryside setting. Avon Hill Lodge and Bruce’s Barn serves as a premier destination for weddings, rehearsal dinners, corporate retreats, group events, meetings, social gatherings, family reunions, and much more. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Joceyn O'Twitchell, jocelyn@avonhilllodge.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>Yes <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Howardsville

All sheets and quilts (and blankets if used) are cleaned between every guest. We daresay you won't find many, if any, other accommodations where the quilts are washed every single time. We are wonderfully situated between Wintergreen and all the breweries, cideries and wineries in the Rockfish Valley. It's easy to access the fun activities offered at Wintergreen and still enjoy all that the craft brewing trail has to offer when you stay here. We are secluded away from the road, but the cabins are close but not too close to each other. You can enjoy the solitude of your own cabin or interact with the other cabins - perfect for friends' getaways, family reunions, birthday celebrations, etc. We have a wonderful communal fire pit area with plenty of firewood and comfortable chairs to enjoy corn hole, a bottle of wine, or local craft beers. LISTING 26: BEECH GROVE FARMHOUSE
Type: Cabins & Cottages
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The Beech Grove Farmhouse is 3 bedroom, 2 bath, early century farmhouse situated in the foothills of the Blue Ridge mountains. There is a sprawling two acre private yard, fire pit, hammock and swing, with beautiful mountain views and is across from Devils Backbone Brewery and Distillery. Located just five miles from the Wintergreen Resort, one mile from Bold Rock Cidery, Blue Toad Cidery and is along Nelson 151 Trails. The Appalachian Trail and other scenic trails are all around the area. Located inside the farmhouse is a fully equipped kitchen, dining room, mud room, laundry room and a cozy living room. WiFi service is provided for our guests.The primary bedroom is located on the first floor with the other two bedrooms on the second floor. Each has ample closet space and a tv with a dvd player. There is also parking available for multiple cars. Listed on the National Register of Historic Places by the National Park Service and recognized as a Virginia Treasure, the home has been thoughtfully updated to provide modern amenities while preserving its historic charm, including lower ceilings and cozy spaces. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Wendy &amp; Jason Harris, harrisx4@icloud.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>No <strong>Located in </strong>Roseland

BEFORE (Original Content):

<div class='fusion-text fusion-text-1 fusion-text-no-margin'> <strong>Miles:</strong> 0.8 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Difficult <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://www.google.com/maps/dir//37.910033,-78.935035/@37.910469,-79.0048087,12z'>Google Maps</a>Google Maps </div> Blackrock Trail is located on a south-facing slope, which promotes the dry, thin soils along the pedlar gneiss rock formations. Mountain laurel, blueberry, and rose azalea thrive in this harsh environment and are in bloom during June. The dominant tree species found along the trail are chestnut oak and table mountain pine; note the way the southwest winds shape these pines. It connect to the Pedlars Edge Trail. <strong>Use caution in winter. </strong>

LISTING 41: BRIMSTONE TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 0.8 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Difficult <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/oEhTdEWe9RmN78wN8'>https://goo.gl/maps/oEhTdEWe9RmN78wN8</a>Google Maps The steep and rocky cliffs provide incredible views of Three Ridges Mountain and points to the southwest. The trail meanders below the Plunge and consists of a series of steep, short up and down sections. Mountain laurel and rhododendron cover these dry soiled slopes, along with chestnut oaks. These thickets are interrupted by “rock gardens” where rock cap fern &amp; fly poison lily grow. <strong>Use caution in winter.</strong>

Welcome to The Bungalow at Afton Mountain Retreat in the heart of Brewery Trail/Route 151, Vineyard country and the foothills of the Blue Ridge Mountains. LISTING 52: CHESTNUT SPRINGS TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 1.1 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Yellow-Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Moderate <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/fpNe9zxvjvkMc2Ks9'>https://goo.gl/maps/fpNe9zxvjvkMc2Ks9</a>Google Maps The north end of the trail leaves the stream and yellow birches and rhododendron of the Shamokin Gorge and enters oak-hickory forest type. As the trail enters south facing territory, several small streams cross the trail. This southern end is ideal for spring wildflowers like bloodroot, wild ginger, and violets; all of which bloom in April. Watch for stinging nettle (long pants recommended). <strong>Use caution in winter.</strong>

Experience the Blue Ridge Mountains and country life of central Virginia and enjoy everything Nelson County has to offer. The Cottage on the Knoll is a comfortable 3br/2bth that’s centrally located in Nelson County, offering the perfect mix of modern comfort and country charm. The Nelson 29 and Nelson 151 craft beverage trails await you, while plenty of hiking, fishing, and mountain views will appease any outdoor recreation lover or nature enthusiast, making it the perfect getaway. <strong>The space:</strong> This three bedroom / two bathroom home features a bright and comfortable main area that includes a large kitchen area and living space that is fully stocked with all amenities. Adjacent to the main living area are two bedrooms featuring queen beds and a full bathroom. A large recreation room downstairs includes a ping pong table, a selection of board games, a mounted TV, and extra seating for fun nights in. The third bedroom with king sized bed and full bath is located downstairs beside the recreation room. After a day in the mountains, cook a meal in your fully stocked kitchen and kick back and enjoy your evenings outside, where you’ll find a wood fire pit in the yard and a gas fireplace and hot tub on the large covered porch. High-speed internet and a smart TV with limited channel access will allow you to connect to your own streaming accounts. Private in-ground swimming pool is available for cottage guests and apartment guests to use during your stay. We will gladly heat the pool during your visit for an additional cost of $25 per day during cooler months. Please indicate if you would like to have the pool heated during your stay at the time of booking so the costs can be adjusted. ** Please note the pool could meet the max number of 8 people if both rentals use it at the same time ** If you would like the pool to yourself during your stay, reach out for additional cost and availability. Whole house centrally located with private hot tub and pool. 30 min away from Charlottesville, Lynchburg, Waynesboro and the top of Wintergreen Resort. Minutes away from Oak Ridge and Lovingston Winery. Grab a cup of coffee and take a morning stroll down one of our 3 out and back trails on the property. Take a picnic lunch in the meadow. And enjoy a glass of wine on your private 2nd floor deck after a day skiing in Wintergreen or hiking the Virginia Blue Ridge Railway Trail. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Bobbi Comer, alwaysrunnin2@gmail.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>No <strong>Located in </strong>Lovingston

LISTING 74: DEVILS BACKBONE CAMP
Type: Campgrounds & Glamping
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Devils Backbone Camp offers an unparalleled camping experience in the heart of the Blue Ridge Mountains, combining a causal camp experience with world-class craft beverages and food. With a range of sites, from full-hook-up RV to rustic wooded tent sites, we offer a range of camping options for every type of adventurer. Nestled in a 5-acre bier garden featuring yard games, numerous seating options, and seasonal live music, our Basecamp Brewpub is open daily with 16+ taps, a full bar, and a seasonal menu featuring smoked meats and something for your whole family to enjoy. In addition to our Basecamp Indoor Brewpub, we have our craft Distillery Lounge, Shanty Cigar Lounge, Summit merchandise shop &amp; Meadows Outdoor Bar &amp; Outdoor grill on property to enjoy as well. Centrally located in the Rockfish Valley, Devils Backbone Camp is within a short drive of some of Virginia’s best hiking, golf, and skiing. Only 5 miles to the Skyline Drive and the Reid's Gap trailhead on the Appalachian Trail, whether exploring by foot or on two wheels, you won’t find any shortage of paths or stunning views. Positioned on the 'Nelson 151' route, Devils Backbone Camp is the prime home base to explore our neighboring breweries, cideries, wineries, and distilleries. With over 20 craft beverage producers within a 20-minute drive, you will find something to suit everyone's tastes. The village of Nellysford offers local dining, shopping a farmers’ market, and a day trip to Charlottesville or Staunton provides extensive shopping, dining, and cultural opportunities. Devils Backbone Camp is the perfect place to come and Drink Outdoors Together. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Jessica Carter, jessica.carter@dbbrewingcompany.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>Yes <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Roseland

LISTING 76: DEVILS KNOB TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 0.6 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Yellow-Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Moderate <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/dqBDS8tZ5xiCsKea8'>https://goo.gl/maps/dqBDS8tZ5xiCsKea8</a>Google Maps The trail begins at the Blue Ridge Overlook. The rock areas are called catoctin greenstone. Note the natural “gardens” along the rock faces and crevices. During the spring, small flowered phacelia and saxifrage, white flowers in the rock crevices, are in full bloom blanketing many parts of the trail. Bird watching and views of Pond Hollow and Fortunes Ridge are the treats of this rocky-footed hike. <strong>Use caution in winter. </strong>

AFTER (Cleaned & Formatted):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 0.6 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Yellow-Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Moderate <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/dqBDS8tZ5xiCsKea8'>https://goo.gl/maps/dqBDS8tZ5xiCsKea8</a> Google Maps The trail begins at the Blue Ridge Overlook. The rock areas are called catoctin greenstone. Note the natural “gardens” along the rock faces and crevices. During the spring, small flowered phacelia and saxifrage, white flowers in the rock crevices, are in full bloom blanketing many parts of the trail. Bird watching and views of Pond Hollow and Fortunes Ridge are the treats of this rocky-footed hike. <strong>Use caution in winter. </strong>

Mountaintop overlook with jaw dropping views and camp themed interior design. Perched upon Elk Mountain just off the Blue Ridge Parkway this petite chalet is under 30 min from Charlottesville, 10 min to 151 vineyards/breweries/cideries, and 10 min to Waynesboro. Relax in this natural retreat featuring 2 king bedrooms, 2 person soaker tub, double headed shower, and ample kitchen w/many extras. Take in the views from the large deck, firepit, under porch swing, or mountainside adirondack chairs. Primarily one level home with sleeping loft. Main floor consists of open concept kitchen, dining, living with electric fireplace. Full bathroom with dual head shower has jack and jill access off the hallway and primary bedroom. Main floor primary bedroom has king bed, walk in closet, and large soaker tub. Laundry is located in the primary bedroom. Upstairs there is a sleeping loft with another king bed. Deck is on the back of the house accessed from the main living/dining areas. From the deck you can see Afton Mountain Vineyard, Veritas, Blue Mountain Brewery, Hazy Mountain and even across to Mont Alto on a clear day. There are approximately 4 steps to get into the front door from the gravel driveway. The one Roku TV is located in the living room. This property was designed for adult guests so anyone wanting to bring children should keep in mind that the property is quite steep and the deck is high off the ground. Note- no ice maker. 4WD or AWD highly recommended in winter- all roads are paved but snow or ice can be prevalent in winter and provide a challenge to 2WD vehicles. While our private road can be accessed directly off the Blue Ridge Parkway we advise arriving via Howardsville TPK to avoid road closures. Weather should always be checked before heading up the mountain as fog can limit visibility. The house does have a generator that kicks on in case of power outage. High speed fiber internet provided. LISTING 85: FISHING AT LAKE NELSON
Type: Fishing
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<a href='https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/waterbody/lake-nelson/'>dgif.virginia.gov</a>Lake Nelson is a 40-acre impoundment located in Nelson County, Virginia. This reservoir is owned by the Department of Wildlife Resources and is managed primarily for fisheries related activities. Facilities available at this reservoir are a boat ramp, parking area and portable toilets which are available March-September. Fishermen are likely to catch largemouth bass, channel catfish, black crappie, bluegill, and redear sunfish. The lake is open 24 hours a day. Outboard motor use is prohibited but electric motors are permitted. (From Arrington, Rt. 655 East (1.4); Left on Rt. 812 (.8) to Concrete ramp) <em>Department of Wildlife Resources requires an Access Permit for visitors to department-owned Wildlife Management Areas (WMAs) and public fishing lakes effective January 1, 2012, who are age 17 and older, unless they possess a valid Virginia hunting, freshwater fishing, or trapping license, or a current Virginia boat registration.</em>

AFTER (Cleaned & Formatted):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

LISTING 88: FISHING THE SOUTH ROCKFISH RIVER
Type: Fishing
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The Rockfish River (South) is stocked with catchable-sized trout periodically by the Department of Wildlife Resources from October through May. See the <a href='https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/fishing/trout-stocking-schedule/'>dgif.virginia.gov</a>TROUT STOCKING SCHEDULE for their stocking plan. Virginia Fishing License required. Trout license also required between October 1 and June 15. Access Points: put-in by the bridge on Route 6 and take-out at the Nelson County Wayside on Rt. 29.

LISTING 89: FISHING THE TYE RIVER
Type: Fishing
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The Tye River is a 35.4-mile-long tributary of the James River in central Virginia originating on the eastern slope of the Blue Ridge Mountains in Nelson County, and is fed by the Piney and Buffalo rivers. It joins with the James River near Norwood, in Nelson County, VA. The Tye River is home to dozens of species of fish and other aquatic life, including largemouth bass, smallmouth bass and bluegill. Browns, rainbows and brooks can be taken on a fly by the studious angler. The Tye River is also stocked with catchable-sized trout periodically by the Virginia Department of Wildlife Resources from October through May. (See the <a href='https://dwr.virginia.gov/fishing/fish-stocking/'>dwr.virginia.gov</a>Trout Stocking Plan and Schedule) Virginia Fishing License required. Trout license also required between October 1 and June 15. Public Access points to the Tye River include along Route 56 bridges, from the bridge on Cub Creek Rd. off Rt. 56… however be aware that permission may be needed from private property owners for nearby car parking.

AFTER (Cleaned & Formatted):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The Tye River is a 35.4-mile-long tributary of the James River in central Virginia originating on the eastern slope of the Blue Ridge Mountains in Nelson County, and is fed by the Piney and Buffalo rivers. It joins with the James River near Norwood, in Nelson County, VA. The Tye River is home to dozens of species of fish and other aquatic life, including largemouth bass, smallmouth bass and bluegill. Browns, rainbows and brooks can be taken on a fly by the studious angler. The Tye River is also stocked with catchable-sized trout periodically by the Virginia Department of Wildlife Resources from October through May. (See the <a href='https://dwr.virginia.gov/fishing/fish-stocking/'>dwr.virginia.gov</a> Trout Stocking Plan and Schedule) Virginia Fishing License required. Trout license also required between October 1 and June 15. Public Access points to the Tye River include along Route 56 bridges, from the bridge on Cub Creek Rd. off Rt. 56… however be aware that permission may be needed from private property owners for nearby car parking.

LISTING 90: FORTUNE’S RIDGE TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 1.1 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Moderate to Difficult <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/JrGreyCFYCzFSGot6'>https://goo.gl/maps/JrGreyCFYCzFSGot6</a>Google Maps Fortunes Ridge Trail follows a cold mountain stream over cascading rock formations. After dropping steeply, a picturesque waterfall emerges about halfway on the trail. The trail passes through laurel thickets that bloom pink in June. Note the dramatic changes in forest types along the trail. Access from Fortunes Ridge Drive at one end or just above Elkwood Drive on Wintergreen Drive at the other. Three Ridges Overlook (via Brimstone Trail access) intersects is another access point as well.

LISTING 106: HIGH LONESOME CABIN
Type: Cabins & Cottages
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
High Lonesome Cabin was built in 2008 in the Virginia Blue Ridge Mountains. This rustic cabin sits on 20 secluded acres high on a steep finger of the Priest Mountain near the Appalachian Trail in Tyro Va., minutes from Crabtree Falls. When you stay at High Lonesome you have a mountain all to yourself, and you may never feel the need to start the car again until you have to leave. But if you do venture out, wineries, orchards, stables, golf, rivers, antiques, lakes and trails are all within minutes of the long gravel driveway. The main attraction of the High Lonesome is definitely outdoors, but the inside is pretty cool, too. There's a porch with a great view, picnic table, ceiling fan, rustic natural log columns, rocking chairs and outdoor speakers....or just listen to the birds and the Tye river off in the distance. Moving inside, there's a small table for dining or cards, a dartboard, a wood stove/ fireplace, plenty of seating to watch a movie on the smart TV, gorgeous stonework with cedar and pine walls/floors, a decent sized kitchen with cookware and utensils provided, small bathroom with shower only. In the back is a small but cozy master bedroom with a very comfortable queen-sized bed. Both areas have ceiling fans and there is a wall mounted heat pump unit for AC/heat. Up the spiral staircase is the loft with a queen-size and a double bed...there's a TV with dvd player and a Gamecube and lots of video games. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Robb Torgler, highlonesomecabin@gmail.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Tyro

LISTING 108: HIGHLANDS LEISURE TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 0.5 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Yellow <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Easy <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/nBaFmA7ogwoGbGA4A'>https://goo.gl/maps/nBaFmA7ogwoGbGA4A</a>Google Maps The Highlands Leisure Trail is the popular connecting trail between the resort’s Blackrock Village and the Wintergarden. The trail provides hikers with quick access to older-growth forests without taking them on a long hike, although in places it can be as steep and rocky as any of the moderate mountain trails. Near the higher end of the trail there is an overlook platform with views of the Highlands ski slopes and Wintergreen’s “Open Space” on Crawford’s Knob and alongside Humpback Mountain.

LISTING 118: LAKE NELSON
Type: Canoeing
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<a href='https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/waterbody/lake-nelson/'>dgif.virginia.gov</a>Lake Nelson is a 40-acre impoundment located in Nelson County, Virginia. This reservoir is owned by the Virginia Department of Game and Inland Fisheries (VDGIF) and is managed primarily for fisheries related activities. Facilities available at this reservoir are a boat ramp, parking area and portable toilets which are available March-September. The lake is open 24 hours a day. Outboard motor use is prohibited but electric motors are permitted. 801 Lake Nelson Lane Arrington, VA 22922 434-263-7015 <strong>Directions</strong> Lake Nelson can be accessed from Route 29 near Colleen (Nelson County), then take Route 655 about 2.8 miles to Arrington; go through Arrington, then left on Route 812, go about 2.2 miles and watch for the sign. Turn left and follow the road to the lake. The <a href='https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/'>https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/</a>Virginia Department of Game and Inland Fisheries (DGIF) requires an Access Permit for visitors to department-owned Wildlife Management Areas (WMAs) and public fishing lakes effective January 1, 2012, who are age 17 and older, unless they possess a valid Virginia hunting, freshwater fishing, or trapping license, or a current Virginia boat registration.

AFTER (Cleaned & Formatted):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<a href='https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/waterbody/lake-nelson/'>dgif.virginia.gov</a> Lake Nelson is a 40-acre impoundment located in Nelson County, Virginia. This reservoir is owned by the Virginia Department of Game and Inland Fisheries (VDGIF) and is managed primarily for fisheries related activities. Facilities available at this reservoir are a boat ramp, parking area and portable toilets which are available March-September. The lake is open 24 hours a day. Outboard motor use is prohibited but electric motors are permitted. 801 Lake Nelson Lane Arrington, VA 22922 434-263-7015 <strong>Directions</strong> Lake Nelson can be accessed from Route 29 near Colleen (Nelson County), then take Route 655 about 2.8 miles to Arrington; go through Arrington, then left on Route 812, go about 2.2 miles and watch for the sign. Turn left and follow the road to the lake. The <a href='https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/'>https://www.dgif.virginia.gov/</a> Virginia Department of Game and Inland Fisheries (DGIF) requires an Access Permit for visitors to department-owned Wildlife Management Areas (WMAs) and public fishing lakes effective January 1, 2012, who are age 17 and older, unless they possess a valid Virginia hunting, freshwater fishing, or trapping license, or a current Virginia boat registration.

Escape to the heart of Virginia’s Blue Ridge Mountains and immerse yourself in nature. Then, retreat to your private mountain cabin or secluded campsite at Love Ridge Mountain Lodging, where relaxation meets adventure. Perched at 2,800 feet, Love Ridge offers breathtaking mountain views and direct access to the Blue Ridge Parkway, just past milepost 16 near Love, Virginia. Whether you’re planning a romantic getaway, family vacation, wedding, corporate retreat, or reunion, Love Ridge provides the perfect setting. Enjoy unparalleled outdoor access, with hiking, biking, and nature trails just steps from your door. With pet-friendly accommodations, even four-legged companions can join the adventure. Onsite, visitors enjoy Virginia Birding and Wildlife Trails, peaceful outdoor spaces, the game shed and outdoor lawn games and starlit nights by the fire, all while being just minutes from Nelson 151’s craft breweries and wineries. Love Ridge is also perfect for groups who want to be together while still enjoying the comforts of their own space. Whether planning a family reunion, wedding, or corporate retreat, guests can stay close in multiple cabins and campsites while having private areas to relax. LISTING 142: NELSON 151
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Located at the base of the Blue Ridge, Route 151 is Main Street for the Rockfish Valley. This northern part of Nelson County is home to 5 wineries, 3 breweries, 1 cidery, cozy Bed &amp; Breakfasts, friendly farmers, spectacular views, and so much more. <a href='https://www.nelson151.com/'>https://www.nelson151.com/</a>ALONG THE TRAIL (north to south) you’ll find: Afton Mountain Vineyards, Veritas Vineyard &amp; Winery, Blue Mountain Brewery, Cardinal Point Vineyard &amp; Winery, Flying Fox Vineyard, Hill Top Berry Farm &amp; Winery, Wild Wolf Brewing Company, Bold Rock Hard Cider and Devils Backbone Brewing Company. Follow the <a href='https://nelson151.com/map/'>https://nelson151.com/map/</a>TRAIL MAP along scenic Route 151 and find out why Nelson County is THE destination for Virginia wine, mead, craft beer, hard cider, local food &amp; outdoor recreation lovers!

LISTING 144: NELSON COUNTY WILDERNESS AREA
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<a href='http://www.wilderness.net/NWPS/wildView?WID=601'>wilderness.net</a>The Wilderness designation provides permanent protection for the two areas in their natural state. <a href='https://www.hikingupward.com/GWNF/ThePriest/'>Hiking Upward</a>The Priest (nearly 6,000 acres) rises from an elevation of about 1,000 feet at the Tye River to 4,063 feet. <a href='https://www.hikingupward.com/gwnf/threeridges/'>Hiking Upward</a>Three Ridges (4,608 acres) is one of the wildest and most rugged areas in the Virginia Blue Ridge. One of the accesses to the area is the <a href='https://appalachiantrail.org/'>https://appalachiantrail.org/</a>Appalachian Trail that runs through this scenic wilderness. <a href='https://nelsoncounty.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/05/Nelson-County-Hiking-Trails-Three-Ridges-Final.pdf'>nelsoncounty.com</a>See the Three Ridges Map

LISTING 149: OLD APPALACHIAN TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 3.3 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Red-Yellow <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Moderate to Difficult <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/ANsHVLfkx6f3PNAaA'>https://goo.gl/maps/ANsHVLfkx6f3PNAaA</a>Google Maps This trail has an array of access points and varying hike lengths. Along the trail there are numerous greenstone ledges that provide spectacular views of the south &amp; Shenandoah Valley. Wildflowers are always abundant in the spring and summer. The trail was once part of the official Appalachian Trail and there is an access trail to the current Appalachian Trail on Laurel Springs Drive near Wolftrap Lane which also connects to the old trail as well. <strong>Use caution in winter.</strong>

LISTING 151: OPEN HEART INN
Type: Cabins & Cottages
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
A historic property nestled on ten acres, Open Heart Inn offers boutique whole house or individual suite rentals in our 4 bedroom/4 bathroom farmhouse and charming cabin. Relax on the back deck, take in the mountain views, and get away from it all. We are tucked away and yet minutes from breweries, trails and more! Originally built in 1840, the farmhouse at Open Heart Inn features four bedrooms, four full bathrooms, a gorgeous kitchen with reclaimed barnwood cabinets, a cozy living room with a wood stove, a yoga studio, and plenty of beautiful outdoor spaces. Located right on the famous Nelson Route 151, we are ideally located for exploring Nelson County hiking trails, wineries, breweries, and cideries, and we are less than 20 minutes to Wintergreen Resort. The farmhouse has the capacity to sleep up to 14 comfortably, and is baby and child friendly. Imagine going back to a simpler time, when you connected over candlelight and gazed at the stars for hours. Open Heart Inn is the ultimate space to unwind, and reconnect with yourself or your loved ones. We purposely do not provide TVs in the suites or cabin, and invite you instead to sit on our many decks, wander our nature path through the back field, or listen to the birds with your morning coffee. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Laura Thibault, openheartinn@gmail.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>No <strong>Located in </strong>Roseland

Service animals allowed. Parrs Camp is a historical rustic fishing cabin restored by us surviving 3 hurricanes dating back to the 1940s. Sleeping 7 there are two bedrooms downstairs; 1Q, 1D and a bath with shower. Upstairs are 3 twin beds and a half bath with TV-VCR movies. Our 70 acre homeplace borders on the George Washing National Forest on the everchanging Tye River that flows just feet away in the front yard. The perfect place to explore away from it all. It offers a charcoal grill, outside fire pit and picnic table area. Firewood is provided. Relax in the hammock while listening to the sound of the river, bird watch , star gaze or watch our goats wander about. With a short drive an abundance of outdoor activities await you; the Blue Ridge Parkway, hike the Appalachian Trail, Spy Rock and Crabtree Falls( highest falls east of the Mississippi River), fish the Tye River, skiing (Wintergreen), submerge in local swimming holes or visit local orchards, vineyards, wineries and breweries. LISTING 161: PEDLARS EDGE TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 1.6 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Difficult <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://www.google.com/maps/dir//37.910033,-78.93503499999997'>Google Maps</a>Google Maps The Pedlars Edge trail connects to the <a href='https://nelsoncounty.com/explore/blackrock-trail/'>nelsoncounty.com</a>'>Blackrock Trail. It is named after the geologic formations found at this elevation–the “pedlar formation,” which was formed 1.1 million years ago. The power-line section is a birdwatchers delight with the creation of edge that increases wildflowers and seeds for birds to feed upon. Allow extra time for access and return. <strong>Use caution in winter.</strong>

LISTING 165: POND HOLLOW TRAIL
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 1.0 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Difficult <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://www.google.com/maps/dir//37.910033,-78.93503499999997'>Google Maps</a>Google Maps Pond Hollow is one of the most interesting and challenging trails on the mountain. It is located within a geologic fault line. The fault line is located in the center of the trail and easily recognized by the 50-100-foot swath of displaced boulders. Note the interesting placement of plant life. The forested area to the west has been labeled as prime bobcat habitat. <strong>Use caution in winter.</strong>

Cabin offers three covered porches and a large open deck all which overlook manicured open fields with two branches of the Tye River and a large stone fire pit. Cabin decor is eclectic with a mixture of old and new collectibles. Comfortable and romantic setting in country style. Cabin is spacious offering three private bedrooms with a semi-private sleeping loft area. Two full baths with two living areas upstairs and down. Fully equipped kitchen and plenty of outdoor setting. It’s a home away from home waiting for you. There are numerous things that make Rock-n-Creek Cabin unique. The cabin sits alone on its own property and you don’t have to share cabin or property with other guest. Secluded and Romantic Setting. Plenty of outdoor space to adventure in. Working artisan studio onsite making waterfowl decoys, Appalachian white oak baskets &amp; kitchen wares. Cooking &amp; Studio Classes available including stay. Unique packages for different occasions like our popular Chick’s Getaway. Conveniently located to local interest like the BRP, Appalachian Trail &amp; Crabtree Falls. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Richard Christy, rchristy.964@gmail.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>Yes <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Montebello

LISTING 178: ROCKFISH VALLEY FARM GUEST HOUSE
Type: Bed and Breakfast
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
This one-story ranch house is 1.5 miles south of Nellysford on RT 151 was initially the home of the manager of Elk Hill Farm. Historically it was the property of the Reids, Colemans and Ewings. It has been farmed for over 250 years. The house, surrounded by pastures and fields dates to just after WWII and was remodeled in the 1990's. Nicely furnished and up to date. The house contains 4 bedrooms, two baths, a very usable living-dining-kitchen area. The porch has two rockers facing the Blue Ridge Mountains, looking directly at Wintergreen Resort on Black Rock Mountain and Devils Knob. The site is unique in that it is surrounded by several waterways and almost 360 degrees of Blue Ridge Mountains. The Rockfish Trails connect to it that lead to swimming holes. There are two donkeys in residence that are very friendly. The property is situated between Bold Rock Cider and Devil's Backbone. One is walkable and the other is just over 1/2 mile away. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Peter Agelasto <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>No <strong>Located in </strong>Nellysford

The kitchen is fully stocked with everything you need to cook and serve your family. There is a coffee/tea bar as well as a counter top ice maker. There is a private bedroom with a queen size bed on the main level. Upstairs in the loft you will find three sleeping areas. The first area has 2 queen size beds, the second area has 2 twin size beds, and the third area has a full size bed with a private balcony over looking the pond. Back downstairs is the single bathroom which also has a full size washer and dryer. Outside you will find a deck that wraps around 2 sides of the house. Featuring a hot tub large enough for the whole crew, many seating areas, outdoor dining, propane and charcoal grills, as well as covered areas. Grab a chair and have a seat next to the private pond. Throw in a fishing line if you brought any along, and maybe have a fire in the fire pit if you are feeling like having s'mores. Two extraordinary homes on one 50 acre family farm. Our handcrafted guest cottage has been one of Nelson County's most beloved rentals for more than a decade, and we're excited to be able to offer the historic 1932 Wintergreen School House as an additional rental on our 50 acre family homestead. Both spaces are extraordinary, carefully curated, rich in local history and art, and designed to be ecologically sensitive. On an 19th C. family farm, the Cottage and School House at Spindle Hill Farm are beautiful, light-filled spaces furnished with unique textiles, antiques, art, and books. Both are in the best part of Nelson County, but very private, on a dead-end road that only serves our farm. The Cottage (sleeps 5) features a handcrafted cedar hot tub, Jotul woodstove, and clawfoot tub, and the historic 1932 School House (sleeps 6) has its original, enormous Rosenwald-style window bank, fireplace, two private ponds, and a gorgeous patio. Every detail has been considered in both, and you'll see why these spaces are destinations unto themselves. Private walking &amp; hiking trails, within walking distance of Devil's Backbone Brewery and Bold Rock, the Rockfish Valley Loop Trail, and minutes from Wintergreen Resort and the Appalachian Trail, our farm is a creative oasis and a one-of-a-kind experience. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Elise Lauterbach, elise.lauterbach@gmail.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>Yes <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Nellysford

When playing the course for the first time, one should be careful to not be distracted by the stunning scenery or wildlife… it may cost them yet another par. Winter golf in Virginia? It’s true. Few other places on earth provide the opportunity to snow ski in the morning and play championship-caliber golf later in the day. Because of a dramatic 3,300 foot change in elevation, it happens at Wintergreen. Route 664 Wintergreen, VA 22958 434-325-8250

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Golf year round in the Rockfish River valley on the 27-hole Rees Jones Design <a href='https://www.wintergreenresort.com/Stoney-Creek/'>wintergreenresort.com</a> Stoney Creek Golf Course. With contoured greens and the occasional side hill lie, Jones brought the natural elements of the landscape into play: meandering streams, hardwood forests and pristine wetlands. When playing the course for the first time, one should be careful to not be distracted by the stunning scenery or wildlife… it may cost them yet another par. Winter golf in Virginia? It’s true. Few other places on earth provide the opportunity to snow ski in the morning and play championship-caliber golf later in the day. Because of a dramatic 3,300 foot change in elevation, it happens at Wintergreen. Route 664 Wintergreen, VA 22958 434-325-8250

LISTING 207: SWANNANOA
Type: Activities
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<p> Swannanoa was built in 1911 by Major James H. Dooley atop the Blue Ridge at Afton Mountain. It is an Italianate marble villa with commanding views of the Shenandoah and Rockfish Valleys. Extensive terraced gardens with statues and marble walls surround the palace. It has been used as a retreat, a clubhouse, and a philosophy institute and is presently under restoration by the owner.</p> <p>It is only open to the public on selected weekends during the spring, summer and fall. Schedule available at Nelson County Visitor Center: <a href='tel:434-263-7015'>434-263-7015</a>. 497 Swannanoa Lane<br/> Afton, Virginia 22920<br/> <a href='tel:540-942-5201'>540-942-5201</a> </p>

AFTER (Cleaned & Formatted):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<p> Swannanoa was built in 1911 by Major James H. Dooley atop the Blue Ridge at Afton Mountain. It is an Italianate marble villa with commanding views of the Shenandoah and Rockfish Valleys. Extensive terraced gardens with statues and marble walls surround the palace. It has been used as a retreat, a clubhouse, and a philosophy institute and is presently under restoration by the owner.</p>

2 bed, 1 bath country cottage in the heart of wine and beer country located in Afton 'proper'. This quaint cottage sits on 1.55 fenced-in acres (bring the pups) with incredible mountain views and a location that is hard to beat. Whether you are coming for the wine/beer hopping, a vineyard wedding (Veritas is a 3 min drive), or to get outdoors (Blueridge Tunnel Trail- 3 min, Shenandoah National Park - 15 min), Sweet Afton is perfect retreat! High speed, 1gig internet in the rural country. Sweet Afton is a historic cottage, yet recently updated with modern amenities (central heating and air, updated kitchen appliances) and tasteful new furnishings provide a fresh, relaxing and bucolic atmosphere. The plot has well maintained gardens surrounding the house and walkways, a hammock, a fire pit with 6 chairs and two porches to enjoy a morning coffee or evening glass of wine with company. Upon the entry to the cottage (enter from driveway in the rear) there is a full kitchen and all of the cooking essentials you will need. Off of the kitchen is a spacious dining room (seats 6) with large bay windows providing beautiful mountain views towards the southwest. From the dining room, a central hallway leads through to the front of the house. Off the hallway, are both bedrooms (queen beds), the full bath and an inviting living room with a pull-out couch (king but also can be a double), a TV and a desk. All of the rooms feel very spacious due to the 10 ft ceilings and natural light from the large windows. On the front of the house is a covered porch with six rocking chairs and a gas grill. Large bushes in front of the house provide privacy from the nearby road. Truly a perfect retreat with everything you need and a great location. **There is a $75 pet cleaning fee per dog - max 2 dogs** This quaint cottage sits on 1.55 fenced-in acres (bring the pups) with incredible mountain views and a location that is hard to beat whether you are coming for the wine/beer hopping, a vineyard wedding (Veritas Winery is a 3 min drive), or to get outdoors (Blue Ridge Tunnel Trail - 3 min, Shenandoah National Park - 15 min). LISTING 213: THE PLUNGE
Type: Hikes & Trails
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
<strong>Miles:</strong> 0.2 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Yellow <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Moderate (if you take your time) <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/1p9jDyMyTjJqYpvo6'>https://goo.gl/maps/1p9jDyMyTjJqYpvo6</a>Google Maps One of the shortest trails on the mountain (it is an access trail to the <a href='https://nelsoncounty.com/explore/blackrock-trail/'>nelsoncounty.com</a>'>Blackrock Trail), The Plunge is an excellent introduction to the beauty and natural history of the area. The hikers will find a steep climb around rock formations that were created millions of years ago (called pedlar gneiss). The Plunge provides an incredible overlook and family picture spot at both the top (at Blackrock Park on Blackrock Circle) and the bottom of the trail. Use extra caution in winter.

Three Sisters Cabin is an historic, pre-Civil War log cabin; one of only a few surviving 2-story log cabins along Virginia's scenic Blue Ridge Parkway. 3 Sisters is the only VA property featured in 'The Top 100 Most Beautiful Rustic Vacation Properties in North America'. We invite you to relax and recharge in the cabin, sauna and hot tub because you deserve it! Lay in the comfy QUEEN sized bed to stargaze at the beautifully lit sky through the skylight. Two TWIN beds as well. Sleeps 4. Three Sisters, set on a private, serene property, was built in 1840. It boasts a hot tub and sauna to help you relax and forget your troubles. BEFORE (Original Content):

<strong>Miles:</strong> 0.8 <strong>Blaze Color:</strong> Red <strong>Difficulty Level:</strong> Difficult <strong>Directions:</strong> <a href='https://goo.gl/maps/worqnjvdmccjF1MJ9'>https://goo.gl/maps/worqnjvdmccjF1MJ9</a>Google Maps One of the more beautiful and challenging hikes on the mountain, the Gorge Trail starts on Laurel Springs Drive near Wolftrap Lane where it follows and crosses the headwaters of Stoney Creek at several spots. An 1870 Farmhouse - 6 Bdrms - 5 BA on the property of WildManDan Brewery. LISTING 243: WINTERGREEN RESORT FISHING
Type: Fishing

BEFORE (Original Content):

Nestled in 11,000 acres in the Blue Ridge Mountains, <a href='http://www.wintergreenresort.com/'>http://www.wintergreenresort.com/</a>WINTERGREEN RESORT is in close proximity to some of the best fly fishing Virginia has to offer. Wintergreen’s Fly Fishing services are designed to accommodate the inexperienced to the expert angler. Wintergreen’s Orvis-Endorsed instructors have the perfect classroom setting at Lake Monocan to introduce students to the art of Fly Fishing, or anglers can experience the thrill of fishing for trout in the mountains or smallmouth bass on the legendary James River with a knowledgeable and friendly guide. Due to limited availability, please call well in advance for bookings. Wintergreen offers half and full day Fly Fishing Classes. Fly-casting instruction is based on The Orvis Progressive Method to Fly Casting and inexperienced to expert casters are welcome. All instruction includes the use of Orvis fly rods and equipment, and all classes are held at the facilities at Lake Monocan and on the private ponds in Stoney Creek. For more information regarding scheduling and rates, call 434-325-8180 or visit <a href='http://www.wintergreenresort.com/'>http://www.wintergreenresort.com/</a>www.wintergreenresort.com | <a href='http://mossycreekflyfishing.com/portfolio-item/stoney-creek/'>mossycreekflyfishing.com</a>www.mossycreekflyfishing.com

====================================================================================================
LISTING 249: WOODSON'S MILL HOUSE
Type: Whole House Rentals
====================================================================================================

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Conveniently located near Charlottesville, Wintergreen, and the Blue Ridge Pkwy, this idyllic Craftsman style home offers the perfect opportunity for couples weekends filled with excursions to local wineries, breweries, and restaurants, or quiet family getaways spent exploring the property's woods, lake, and historic Woodson’s Mill. This property offers the best of both worlds and is centrally located to all that the heart of the Blue Ridge Mountains has to offer. Woodson’s Mill, built in 1794, placed on the National Registry of Historic Places on December 17, 1992 is located on the property. It is an excellent example of a wood framed, water-powered grist mill that is not only structurally intact, but still in operation. Deep Roots Milling, a three man operation, rents the mill one week per month to grind local grains for their customers. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>William &amp; Charlene Chinworth, bandcchinworth@gmail.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Roseland

Type: Cabins & Cottages

BEFORE (Original Content):
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Wren House is a 5 bedroom 3 1/2 bath country home with a 4.95 rating on Air BnB. The house is very private with lawns, secluded wooded surroundings and views of pastures, sunsets and the mountains. You will have it all to yourself. Birds and nature abound at Wren House. We are close to 151 wineries, breweries, hiking and cultural sites. Charlottesville is only 35 minutes away and Wintergreen is a 20 minute drive up the mountain. 6-7 seat hot tub / spa; Fire pit &amp; Adirondack chairs; Inside fireplace; Recently remodeled kitchen and baths; Room for 12; Pet friendly; New farmhouse kitchen; Spotlessly clean; Luxury quality beds and bedding; Large covered porch with view; Hammock in the oak grove; Large screen smart TV; Great vibes. <em>*Lodging descriptions are provided by the host and not independently verified.</em> <strong>Contact </strong>Tom Fisher, tom@environdc.com <strong>Staff on Site? </strong>No <strong>Pet-Friendly? </strong>Yes <strong>Located in </strong>Afton
